| agent-spend.md:1 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:1` | reworded | # Agent Spend - the unit is the TURN, and it costs about a dollar |
| agent-spend.md:3 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:3` | reworded | Zaal, 2026-08-10: |
| agent-spend.md:3 | `archive/agent-spend.md:3` | archive-only | "I just reupped twice this week, this is unsustainable. |
| agent-spend.md:3 | `archive/agent-spend.md:3` | archive-only | I don't even know what results I'm getting out of these loops. |
| agent-spend.md:3 | `archive/agent-spend.md:3` | archive-only | It said used over $10 and I want to know on what, in less than 24 hours." |
| agent-spend.md:7 | `archive/agent-spend.md:7` | archive-only | Nothing recorded it. |
| agent-spend.md:7 | `archive/agent-spend.md:7` | archive-only | Claude Code writes token counts into its session transcripts but **no dollar figure**, and no ledger existed anywhere on the machine. |
| agent-spend.md:7 | `archive/agent-spend.md:7` | archive-only | Spend was invisible by construction: the only signal was the bill. |
| agent-spend.md:7 | `archive/agent-spend.md:7` | archive-only | `scripts/agents/zao-spend` (also `~/bin/zao-spend`) now prices the transcripts and puts the cost next to the PRs opened in the same window. |
| agent-spend.md:13 | `archive/agent-spend.md:13` | archive-only | ## What the measurement actually showed |
| agent-spend.md:15 | `archive/agent-spend.md:15` | archive-only | Measured over 24h on 2026-08-10, 15 sessions, $1,838 of list-price consumption: |
| agent-spend.md:17 | `archive/agent-spend.md:17` | archive-only | \| Component \| Share \| |
| agent-spend.md:19 | `archive/agent-spend.md:19` | archive-only | \| cache reads (998.5M tokens) \| ~81% \| |
| agent-spend.md:20 | `archive/agent-spend.md:20` | archive-only | \| cache writes (22.3M tokens) \| ~12% \| |
| agent-spend.md:21 | `archive/agent-spend.md:21` | archive-only | \| output generated (2.0M tokens) \| ~8% \| |
| agent-spend.md:23 | `archive/agent-spend.md:23` | archive-only | **Two hypotheses died on contact with the data, and both are worth knowing:** |
| agent-spend.md:25 | `archive/agent-spend.md:25` | archive-only | *"Long sessions get progressively more expensive."* **False.** Per-turn cost |
| agent-spend.md:26 | `archive/agent-spend.md:26` | archive-only | across one 14,021-turn session was flat - $1.07, $1.19, $1.03, $1.04 per turn across its four quarters. |
| agent-spend.md:26 | `archive/agent-spend.md:26` | archive-only | Compaction bounds the context, so turn 14,000 costs what turn 10 costs. |
| agent-spend.md:26 | `archive/agent-spend.md:26` | archive-only | Session LENGTH is a context-conflict problem (`session-boundaries.md`), not a cost problem. |
| agent-spend.md:30 | `archive/agent-spend.md:30` | archive-only | *"The token count is the thing to watch."* **Misleading.** 998M of the ~1.03B |
| agent-spend.md:31 | `archive/agent-spend.md:31` | archive-only | tokens were cache READS, billed at a tenth of input. |
| agent-spend.md:31 | `archive/agent-spend.md:31` | archive-only | A huge token number is mostly evidence of a warm cache, not of waste. |
| agent-spend.md:34 | `archive/agent-spend.md:34` | archive-only | **The finding that survived: cost = turns × ~$1.01.** Flat, predictable, and almost entirely independent of how much you accomplish in each one. |
| agent-spend.md:37 | `archive/agent-spend.md:37` | archive-only | ## What this changes (behavior-changing) |
| agent-spend.md:41 | `archive/agent-spend.md:41` | archive-only | - **Batch tool calls.** Three independent `Bash` calls in one turn cost what one |
| agent-spend.md:42 | `archive/agent-spend.md:42` | archive-only | costs. |
| agent-spend.md:42 | `archive/agent-spend.md:42` | archive-only | Issuing them across three turns costs triple for identical output. |
| agent-spend.md:42 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:7` | reworded | Any independent reads - a status check, a file read, a `gh` query - go in ONE turn. |
| agent-spend.md:44 | `archive/agent-spend.md:44` | archive-only | - **Polling loops are the most expensive way to wait.** A `/loop` tick that checks |
| agent-spend.md:45 | `archive/agent-spend.md:45` | archive-only | state and finds nothing changed still costs $6-10, because it is 6-10 turns. |
| agent-spend.md:45 | `archive/agent-spend.md:45` | archive-only | Five ticks that report "nothing changed" is $40 for a sentence. |
| agent-spend.md:45 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:8` | reworded | If the thing being waited on is a HUMAN action (a merge, a decision, a keypress), do not poll at all - stop the loop and let the human re-start it. |
| agent-spend.md:45 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:8` | reworded | A loop is for work that arrives on its own, never for work that arrives when Zaal gets to it. |
| agent-spend.md:50 | `archive/agent-spend.md:50` | archive-only | - **Back off hard on quiet.** `agent-loops.md` rule 5 says empty queue = zero |
| agent-spend.md:51 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:9` | reworded | Now it has a number: two consecutive no-change ticks means stop, not lengthen. |
| agent-spend.md:51 | `archive/agent-spend.md:51` | archive-only | Lengthening a pointless loop still pays for it, just later. |
| agent-spend.md:53 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:10` | reworded | - **The expensive mistake is the re-check, not the big read.** Reading a 400-line |
| agent-spend.md:54 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:7` | reworded | file once is one turn. |
| agent-spend.md:54 | `archive/agent-spend.md:54` | archive-only | Reading four 100-line files across four turns is four. |
| agent-spend.md:54 | `archive/agent-spend.md:54` | archive-only | Prefer one wide look over several narrow ones, which inverts the usual instinct to "just check one more thing". |
| agent-spend.md:58 | `archive/agent-spend.md:58` | archive-only | ## What did NOT change |
| agent-spend.md:60 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:11` | reworded | - Reading files, running tests, and verifying claims are the WORK. |
| agent-spend.md:61 | `archive/agent-spend.md:61` | archive-only | licence to skip verification to save turns - a wrong claim shipped to production costs more than any number of checks (`confirm-before-claiming-absence.md`, which explicitly says burn the cap to be certain about groun... |
| agent-spend.md:64 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:11` | reworded | - The lesson is to spend turns on *finding things out*, not on *asking whether |
| agent-spend.md:65 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:11` | reworded | anything happened yet*. |
| agent-spend.md:67 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:12` | reworded | ## Keeping it visible |
| agent-spend.md:69 | `archive/agent-spend.md:69` | archive-only | `zao-spend` runs offline in a second and answers the question that had no answer: |
| agent-spend.md:72 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:12` | reworded | zao-spend              # last 24h, by session, with the PRs it bought |
| agent-spend.md:73 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:12` | reworded | zao-spend --days 7     # the week |
| agent-spend.md:74 | `archive/agent-spend.md:74` | archive-only | zao-spend --by-lane    # grouped by working directory |
| agent-spend.md:77 | `archive/agent-spend.md:77` | archive-only | It prints cost per PR and cost per 1k output tokens, so a window with real spend and no output is visible rather than inferred. |
| agent-spend.md:77 | `.claude/rules/agent-spend.md:12` | reworded | On Max these are list-price consumption figures, not invoice amounts - a meter, not a bill. |

| anti-fabrication.md:3 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:3` | archive-only | Subagents and long loops fabricate: they claim files were written that were not, grade findings "critical" that are intentional, invent numbers/URLs/paths, and report a plan as if it were done. |
| anti-fabrication.md:3 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:3` | archive-only | This rule makes fabrication expensive to commit and cheap to catch. |
| anti-fabrication.md:3 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:3` | archive-only | It exists because in one session two subagents claimed file-writes that did not exist and one over-graded 8 findings to what was really 1. |
| anti-fabrication.md:9 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:9` | archive-only | ## The one principle |
| anti-fabrication.md:11 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:3` | reworded | **A claim is not a fact until it is checked against ground truth.** The orchestrator (the loop / the main agent) VERIFIES every load-bearing claim a subagent makes before trusting it, reporting it, or opening a PR fro... |
| anti-fabrication.md:11 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:11` | archive-only | "The rule has been written to X", "8 critical bugs", "the endpoint returns Y" are claims - `ls` the file, `grep` the line, re-read the source, re-fetch the URL. |
| anti-fabrication.md:17 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:17` | archive-only | ## Rules (behavior-changing) |
| anti-fabrication.md:20 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:5` | reworded | orchestrator writes it, at the right path/number, and reads back the diff. |
| anti-fabrication.md:20 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:20` | archive-only | Subagent file-writes collide (a doc landed at a colliding low number), get left uncommitted in the shared tree, and can be claimed-but-absent. |
| anti-fabrication.md:20 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:5` | reworded | If a subagent must write (worktree isolation), it reports the EXACT path and the orchestrator `ls`-confirms it before proceeding. |
| anti-fabrication.md:26 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:6` | reworded | **Every finding carries EVIDENCE or is marked UNVERIFIED.** Require subagents |
| anti-fabrication.md:27 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:6` | reworded | to return each finding as `{claim, file:line (or URL), evidence-quote}`. |
| anti-fabrication.md:27 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:6` | reworded | A finding with no quotable evidence is `UNVERIFIED` - never reported as fact, never graded as critical. |
| anti-fabrication.md:31 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:7` | reworded | **The orchestrator re-checks every high-stakes claim before it leaves the |
| anti-fabrication.md:32 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:7` | reworded | loop.** For each "critical"/"confirmed"/security finding: open the cited file:line and confirm the quote is really there and means what was claimed. |
| anti-fabrication.md:32 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:32` | archive-only | Tonight this downgraded 8 "critical" to 1 real decision. |
| anti-fabrication.md:32 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:3` | reworded | Do it every time. |
| anti-fabrication.md:36 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:36` | archive-only | Fabricated alarm is worse than a miss.** Default a |
| anti-fabrication.md:37 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:8` | reworded | finding to the lowest severity the evidence supports. |
| anti-fabrication.md:37 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:37` | archive-only | "Intentional soft-fail with a comment saying so" is not "critical data-loss bug." Say what the code actually does, then whether it is a problem. |
| anti-fabrication.md:42 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:9` | reworded | counts.** Every number in a report traces to a measurement (a query, a `wc -l`, a fetched page). |
| anti-fabrication.md:42 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:9` | reworded | A doc number comes from the reservation scan, not a guess. |
| anti-fabrication.md:42 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:9` | reworded | A URL is only cited if it was actually fetched (mark FULL/PARTIAL/ FAILED). |
| anti-fabrication.md:42 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:42` | archive-only | Unknown = write "unknown", not a plausible value. |
| anti-fabrication.md:47 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:10` | reworded | **Distinguish DONE from PLANNED in every report.** "Shipped", "merged", |
| anti-fabrication.md:48 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:10` | reworded | "applied", "fixed" require proof (a PR number that exists, a green run, a verified row). |
| anti-fabrication.md:48 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:10` | reworded | If it is a design or a proposal, say "design/spec, not built". |
| anti-fabrication.md:52 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:11` | reworded | **Standard grounding preamble for every audit/research subagent prompt:** |
| anti-fabrication.md:59 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | ## The orchestrator's verify checklist (run before any loop artifact ships) |
| anti-fabrication.md:61 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | - Claimed a file was written? |
| anti-fabrication.md:61 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:3` | reworded | `ls` it. |
| anti-fabrication.md:61 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | Absent = the claim is false; author it yourself. |
| anti-fabrication.md:62 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | - Claimed a file:line? |
| anti-fabrication.md:62 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:62` | archive-only | `grep`/`sed` it. |
| anti-fabrication.md:63 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | - A "critical"/security finding? |
| anti-fabrication.md:63 | `archive/anti-fabrication.md:63` | archive-only | Read the source; confirm the quote + its meaning. |
| anti-fabrication.md:64 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | - A number/URL/doc-number? |
| anti-fabrication.md:64 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | Traced to a real measurement/fetch/scan? |
| anti-fabrication.md:64 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:3` | reworded | If not, remove it. |
| anti-fabrication.md:65 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:10` | reworded | - "Done/shipped/merged"? |
| anti-fabrication.md:65 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:10` | reworded | Proof exists (PR#, green run, verified row)? |
| anti-fabrication.md:65 | `.claude/rules/anti-fabrication.md:14` | reworded | If not, say "not verified". |

| capture-quality.md:8 | `archive/capture-quality.md:8` | archive-only | ## The rule (behavior-changing, both capture ends) |
| capture-quality.md:11 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:5` | reworded | ZOE holds it and asks ONE button question - "Add a why" / "Board it bare" (the gate prompts, never blocks; bare-on-purpose is one tap). |
| capture-quality.md:11 | `archive/capture-quality.md:11` | archive-only | Implementation: |
| capture-quality.md:16 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:6` | reworded | **Organizer / any lane routing cards to the board (terminal end):** a card |
| capture-quality.md:17 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:6` | reworded | routed to the board carries WHY + DONE-WHEN synthesized from the context it came from. |
| capture-quality.md:17 | `archive/capture-quality.md:17` | archive-only | A card where NEITHER is derivable goes to GRILL-QUEUE.md instead of the board - a grill question is the right home for "what even is this", the board is not. |
| capture-quality.md:22 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:7` | reworded | **Tooling floor:** `zao-tracker` create subcommands already take `--why` |
| capture-quality.md:23 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:7` | reworded | and `--done-when` - use them on every card a session creates. |
| capture-quality.md:23 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:7` | reworded | A card without them should be the exception a human chose, never the default a pipeline produced. |
| capture-quality.md:29 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:9` | reworded | - This gates BOARD writes, not capture itself - capture stays frictionless |
| capture-quality.md:30 | `archive/capture-quality.md:30` | archive-only | (one thought, one line, never lost). |
| capture-quality.md:30 | `archive/capture-quality.md:30` | archive-only | The gate runs where the thought becomes a card. |
| capture-quality.md:32 | `.claude/rules/capture-quality.md:9` | reworded | - Never DROP a capture for missing context (feedback: never drop, always |
| capture-quality.md:33 | `archive/capture-quality.md:33` | archive-only | park) - the choices are enrich, board-bare-on-purpose, or grill-queue. |
| capture-quality.md:37 | `archive/capture-quality.md:37` | archive-only | Card b8cba711; incidents of 2026-08-19 (Sparq / Colleen / NEXUS grill rounds). |

| claude-usage.md:14 | `.claude/rules/claude-usage.md:3` | reworded | **Match each task to the cheapest surface that can do it WELL, and reserve the Claude Code weekly cap for grounded live-code work.** Zaal is on Claude Max - the constraint is not per-token cost, it is the **weekly usa... |

| code-over-inference.md:3 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:3` | archive-only | *"We really need to focus on AI costs versus building software that is just autonomous code that runs for us for free. |
| code-over-inference.md:3 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:3` | archive-only | That's super important distinction that we need to make and include as a part of our fleet organization."* |
| code-over-inference.md:8 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:8` | archive-only | He is right, and our own numbers make the case harder than any argument. |
| code-over-inference.md:10 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:10` | archive-only | ## The measurement, same machine, same day |
| code-over-inference.md:14 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:14` | archive-only | \| 24h of agent work \| **$3,001.85** across 71 sessions, **$56.64 per PR** \| |
| code-over-inference.md:15 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:15` | archive-only | \| `zj`, `zao-wall`, `zao-spend`, `zao-waiting`, `zao-lanes`, `lane-send`, `zao-status-write` - **2,019 lines** \| **$0 per run, forever, unlimited** \| |
| code-over-inference.md:17 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:17` | archive-only | Those 2,019 lines were written BY an agent. |
| code-over-inference.md:17 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:17` | archive-only | That is the whole point: |
| code-over-inference.md:17 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:17` | archive-only | **the inference was paid once and the capability is now free.** `zj` renders the wall forty times a day for nothing. |
| code-over-inference.md:17 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:17` | archive-only | An agent asked to "look at the lanes and tell me which need work" forty times would cost forty turns. |
| code-over-inference.md:22 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:5` | reworded | ## The one test |
| code-over-inference.md:24 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:5` | reworded | Before an agent does anything repeatable, ask: |
| code-over-inference.md:28 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:5` | reworded | - **Same judgment every time** -> it is a SCRIPT. |
| code-over-inference.md:28 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:5` | reworded | Write it once, run it free. |
| code-over-inference.md:29 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:5` | reworded | - **Fresh judgment each time** -> it is inference. |
| code-over-inference.md:29 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:29` | archive-only | Pay for it, and it is worth it. |
| code-over-inference.md:31 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:31` | archive-only | That is the whole rule. |
| code-over-inference.md:31 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:31` | archive-only | Everything below is how to apply it without being silly about it. |
| code-over-inference.md:34 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:7` | reworded | ## What is almost never inference |
| code-over-inference.md:36 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:36` | archive-only | If an agent is doing one of these by reading and reasoning, it is burning a turn on something a script does for nothing: |
| code-over-inference.md:39 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:39` | archive-only | - **Counting, parsing, filtering, sorting.** Board queries, lane states, file |
| code-over-inference.md:40 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:40` | archive-only | inventories, PR lists. |
| code-over-inference.md:41 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:7` | reworded | - **Fetching and transforming.** `curl`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `gh api`, git. |
| code-over-inference.md:42 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:42` | archive-only | - **Formatting and rendering.** Tables, digests, status lines, index rows. |
| code-over-inference.md:43 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:7` | reworded | - **Scheduled checks.** Anything on a timer is a cron, not a loop. |
| code-over-inference.md:44 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:44` | archive-only | - **Transcription.** `whisper.cpp` runs free on the Pi's idle cores. |
| code-over-inference.md:44 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:44` | archive-only | An LLM |
| code-over-inference.md:45 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:45` | archive-only | transcription API bills per minute of audio, forever. |
| code-over-inference.md:46 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:46` | archive-only | - **Verification with a definite answer.** "Does this file exist", "did the test |
| code-over-inference.md:47 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:47` | archive-only | pass", "is this PR mergeable" - assert it, do not reason about it. |
| code-over-inference.md:49 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:8` | reworded | ## What genuinely needs inference |
| code-over-inference.md:51 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:7` | reworded | Do not over-correct. |
| code-over-inference.md:51 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:51` | archive-only | These are worth every turn: |
| code-over-inference.md:53 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:53` | archive-only | - **Judgment under ambiguity** - which of two readings of a request is right. |
| code-over-inference.md:54 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:54` | archive-only | - **Writing that someone will read** - docs, briefs, copy, PR bodies. |
| code-over-inference.md:55 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:55` | archive-only | - **Grounded code changes** - reading live code and editing it correctly. |
| code-over-inference.md:56 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:56` | archive-only | - **Synthesis** - turning a transcript into decisions and obligations. |
| code-over-inference.md:57 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:8` | reworded | - **Deciding what the script should be.** This is the highest-leverage |
| code-over-inference.md:58 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:58` | archive-only | inference there is: an agent spending a turn to write a tool that then runs free a thousand times. |
| code-over-inference.md:61 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:61` | archive-only | ## The counterexample, priced |
| code-over-inference.md:63 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:63` | archive-only | Thirteen VPS cheap-loops ran for a week on a 2-core box at load 12.5 with 2GB of swap, five of them doing concurrent local LLM inference, and **wrote zero files in seven days**. |
| code-over-inference.md:63 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:63` | archive-only | They were agent loops doing work that had no fresh judgment in it - and they paid for it in CPU continuously while producing nothing. |
| code-over-inference.md:68 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:68` | archive-only | Compare `zao-vault-linkrot.py` or `zao-board-vault-check.py`: written once, scan the whole estate in seconds, cost nothing, and their findings are reproducible rather than re-derived. |
| code-over-inference.md:72 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:72` | archive-only | ## How this changes fleet organization |

| code-over-inference.md:74 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:74` | archive-only | Ship the tool, then let the tool produce the result forever. |
| code-over-inference.md:78 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:78` | archive-only | Loops on a timer are cron, not agents.** An agent loop that wakes, checks, and finds nothing changed still costs a full turn (~$1, `agent-spend.md`). |
| code-over-inference.md:78 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:78` | archive-only | A cron that does the same costs nothing. |
| code-over-inference.md:83 | `.claude/rules/code-over-inference.md:13` | reworded | Prefer local binaries over hosted inference for mechanical media work.** `whisper.cpp`, `ffmpeg`, `yt-dlp` on the Pi's idle cores are free and unmetered. |
| code-over-inference.md:83 | `archive/code-over-inference.md:83` | archive-only | This is also why the Pi matters: it adds CPU, and CPU is the cheap resource. |

| first-handler-wins.md:14 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:14` | archive-only | **`build:` never reached the build classifier.** The batch-answer guard in |

| first-handler-wins.md:15 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:7` | reworded | `build:` matched. |

| first-handler-wins.md:15 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:15` | archive-only | It ate the ENTIRE build vocabulary - build, code, fix, ship, implement - because every one is lowercase letters plus a colon. |

| first-handler-wins.md:22 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:22` | archive-only | **A ZOE reply to one comment marked every other comment answered.** |
| first-handler-wins.md:23 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:23` | archive-only | `findUnansweredMentions` treated ANY later ZOE comment as an answer. |
| first-handler-wins.md:23 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:23` | archive-only | But ZOE is not one writer - `task-teammate-ack` also posts as ZOE. |
| first-handler-wins.md:23 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:23` | archive-only | Its ack of Iman's comment landed after Zaal's `@zoe` command and marked that command answered. |
| first-handler-wins.md:23 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:23` | archive-only | The command would never have run. |
| first-handler-wins.md:23 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:23` | archive-only | No error, no log: the queue simply stopped containing it. |
| first-handler-wins.md:29 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:29` | archive-only | **The same board command re-ran every hour.** Found by a sibling loop the |
| first-handler-wins.md:30 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:30` | archive-only | same day (PR #3000) - the mirror image of (2). |
| first-handler-wins.md:30 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:30` | archive-only | Where (2) was "claimed by the wrong handler", this was "never claimed at all", so it stayed eligible forever. |

| first-handler-wins.md:40 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:40` | archive-only | It returns 200, or replies "Logged 1 answer from batch", or exits 0. |
| first-handler-wins.md:40 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:40` | archive-only | Every monitor stays green. |

| first-handler-wins.md:48 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:48` | archive-only | ## The rule (behavior-changing) |
| first-handler-wins.md:53 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:7` | reworded | **Specific before generic, and say so in a comment.** A command prefix |
| first-handler-wins.md:54 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:7` | reworded | (`build:`, `note:`) is more specific than a generic pattern (`^[a-z]+:`). |
| first-handler-wins.md:54 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:7` | reworded | The generic one must EXCLUDE the specific vocabulary, not merely sit after it - because the first match returns. |
| first-handler-wins.md:58 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:8` | reworded | **A handler that RETURNS owns the input. |
| first-handler-wins.md:58 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:8` | reworded | Guard it at the gate.** If a branch |
| first-handler-wins.md:59 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:59` | archive-only | ends the request, a wrong match there is unrecoverable - nothing downstream runs. |
| first-handler-wins.md:59 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:8` | reworded | Put the exclusion on the branch itself, and again inside the function it calls. |
| first-handler-wins.md:59 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:59` | archive-only | Defence at the gate and in the room; the duplication is deliberate. |
| first-handler-wins.md:63 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:9` | reworded | **"Handled" must name WHAT it handled.** Never infer handled-ness from |
| first-handler-wins.md:64 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:64` | archive-only | A reply records the id of the thing it answers (`replyTo`), a poller records the id of what it surfaced. |
| first-handler-wins.md:64 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:64` | archive-only | "Some later message from the same author" is not an answer to THIS message. |
| first-handler-wins.md:68 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:10` | reworded | **Announce the claim.** The handler that consumes an input logs that it did, |
| first-handler-wins.md:69 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:10` | reworded | once (`featureRan`). |
| first-handler-wins.md:69 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:69` | archive-only | Three of today's bugs were diagnosed in minutes only because zero `[zoe/ran]` lines proved the classifier had never executed - distinguishing "ran and declined" from "never reached". |
| first-handler-wins.md:69 | `archive/first-handler-wins.md:69` | archive-only | Without that, you debug the wrong module. |
| first-handler-wins.md:74 | `.claude/rules/first-handler-wins.md:11` | reworded | **A regression test uses the VERBATIM input that failed.** Not a |

| icm-grounding.md:34 | `archive/icm-grounding.md:34` | archive-only | ## ICM is UPSTREAM - generate outward, never drift |
| icm-grounding.md:36 | `.claude/rules/icm-grounding.md:13` | reworded | The box is the source; everything else is downstream and derived FROM it: |
| icm-grounding.md:38 | `.claude/rules/icm-grounding.md:13` | reworded | - A site's `llms.txt`, JSON-LD `schema.org/Organization`, pitch copy, and social |

| icm-grounding.md:39 | `archive/icm-grounding.md:39` | archive-only | Real example (2026-07-27): |
| icm-grounding.md:39 | `archive/icm-grounding.md:39` | archive-only | `zao-website`'s `llms.txt` said "web3 music community and incubator" while the `thezao` box said "decentralized impact network / ZTalent Artist Organization, music-first" - drift, because nothing kept them in sync. |
| icm-grounding.md:39 | `.claude/rules/icm-grounding.md:13` | reworded | Fix: derive downstream copy from the box, and when the truth changes, change the BOX first, then regenerate. |
| icm-grounding.md:45 | `.claude/rules/icm-grounding.md:13` | reworded | - If the box and a downstream surface disagree, the **box wins** - update the surface |
| icm-grounding.md:46 | `.claude/rules/icm-grounding.md:13` | reworded | (or, if the box is what's stale, update the box via the `/icm` skill, then the surface). |
| icm-grounding.md:46 | `archive/icm-grounding.md:46` | archive-only | Never silently trust the drifted copy. |

| idle-lane-audit.md:1 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:1` | reworded | # Idle Lane Audit - a lane with no queue audits its own ground, it does not wait |
| idle-lane-audit.md:3 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:3` | archive-only | Zaal, 2026-08-20, when asked how idle lanes should render on The Wall: |
| idle-lane-audit.md:5 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:3` | reworded | > "idle should prob for an audit on the project and based on dotfiles and |

| idle-lane-audit.md:8 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | archive-only | That is a better answer than any of the display options it was asked about, because it changes what idle IS rather than how it looks. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:8 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | archive-only | A lane with nothing queued has capacity and context - the two expensive things - and spends them on nothing. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:8 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | archive-only | This rule spends them on verification. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:13 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:13` | archive-only | ## Why this specific work, and not "find something useful" |
| idle-lane-audit.md:15 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:15` | archive-only | **Four separate load-bearing things were found missing or broken on 2026-08-20 alone**, every one of them silent until someone happened to look: |
| idle-lane-audit.md:18 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:18` | archive-only | \| What \| How it failed \| Found by \| |
| idle-lane-audit.md:20 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:20` | archive-only | \| `gstack browse/dist/` \| never built, so `/browse` could never run \| two lanes blocked, neither could name why \| |
| idle-lane-audit.md:21 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:21` | archive-only | \| capture-triage-crush CLI \| gone from the Mac, 5 skills still invoke it \| a card, days later \| |
| idle-lane-audit.md:22 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:22` | archive-only | \| `zj` probe \| no ceiling, hung the whole wall on a loaded host \| Zaal, twice \| |
| idle-lane-audit.md:23 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:23` | archive-only | \| `zj` needs-you \| fires on every idle lane, headline number ~4x wrong \| a screenshot \| |
| idle-lane-audit.md:25 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:25` | archive-only | None was caught by a check. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:25 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:25` | archive-only | All were caught by a human hitting them. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:25 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:25` | archive-only | That is the gap idle capacity should be closing, and it is `vanishing-dependencies.md` rule 3 (a dependency's existence is checked, not assumed) finally getting someone to do the checking. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:30 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:30` | archive-only | ## The behavior (behavior-changing) |
| idle-lane-audit.md:32 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:5` | reworded | **A lane that finishes its queue does NOT go quiet and wait. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:32 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:5` | reworded | It runs the audit below, reports what it found, and only then idles.** On The Wall an idle lane is a defect ([[project_the_wall]]); this is the lane's own first response to being one. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:37 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:5` | reworded | Scope it to the lane's OWN project - its repo, its brief, its tools. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:37 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:37` | archive-only | This is not a licence to audit the whole estate; twelve lanes each auditing everything is twelve times the cost and one twelfth the depth. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:43 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:7` | reworded | Read `~/zao-vault/handoffs/<lane>.md` and check it is still TRUE, not just present. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:43 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:7` | reworded | Has the work it describes shipped? |
| idle-lane-audit.md:43 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:7` | reworded | Does it name files, branches, PRs, or card IDs that no longer exist? |
| idle-lane-audit.md:43 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:43` | archive-only | A brief that has drifted is worse than none, because the next session boots on it. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:48 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:48` | archive-only | Dotfiles and tooling - does what we depend on still exist |
| idle-lane-audit.md:50 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | reworded | For every tool the lane's work invokes - a `~/bin` script, a skill, a binary, a build artifact: |
| idle-lane-audit.md:53 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | reworded | - **Does it resolve?** Use `find -L`, not `find`. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:54 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | reworded | `~/bin` are SYMLINKS into `~/zaal-dotfiles`, and a plain `find` reports present files as missing. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:54 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:54` | archive-only | That exact mistake was made on 2026-08-20. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:56 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | reworded | - **Copy the probe verbatim from the caller.** If a script tests |
| idle-lane-audit.md:57 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:57` | archive-only | `command -v mlx_whisper`, test `mlx_whisper` - not `mlx-whisper`, which is the package name. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:57 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:57` | archive-only | Same day, same session, second false negative. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:59 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:59` | archive-only | - **Is it git-tracked?** An untracked file inside a git tree is the worst case: |
| idle-lane-audit.md:60 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:60` | archive-only | it looks safe and git cannot give it back. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:60 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:60` | archive-only | If something depends on it and git does not hold it, that is a finding. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:62 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | reworded | - **Is a build artifact actually built?** `dist/` is correctly gitignored and |
| idle-lane-audit.md:63 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:63` | archive-only | therefore correctly absent on a fresh clone - which is exactly how `/browse` was dead for weeks while looking fine. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:66 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:9` | reworded | Memory and rules - is anything learned still unwritten |
| idle-lane-audit.md:68 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:68` | archive-only | - Did this lane learn something durable that lives only in its transcript? |
| idle-lane-audit.md:69 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:69` | archive-only | repeated bug, a corrected assumption, a tool that behaves unexpectedly. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:69 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:8` | reworded | Rules and memories are the artifact; a transcript is not (`agent-loops.md` rule 6). |
| idle-lane-audit.md:71 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:9` | reworded | - Does an existing memory or rule now contradict what the lane has seen? |

| idle-lane-audit.md:74 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:9` | reworded | - Is anything in a scratchpad that a future session will need? |
| idle-lane-audit.md:74 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:74` | archive-only | Scratchpads hold |
| idle-lane-audit.md:75 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:75` | archive-only | nothing that outlives the session (`handoff-discipline.md` rule 8). |
| idle-lane-audit.md:79 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:79` | archive-only | Report findings even when there are none - "audited, nothing missing" is a result, and an honest clean report is a success rather than a wasted run (`anti-fabrication.md` rule 4). |
| idle-lane-audit.md:79 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:10` | reworded | Findings become a card, a rule PR, or a brief correction. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:79 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:79` | archive-only | **Never a silent fix and never a deletion** - surface the path and why, and let Zaal delete (`no-rm-rf.md`). |
| idle-lane-audit.md:87 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:87` | archive-only | - **Absence claims still need proof.** "X is missing" from this audit carries |

| idle-lane-audit.md:88 | `archive/idle-lane-audit.md:88` | archive-only | Two false negatives were produced in one session by a wrong `find` and a wrong binary name - both would have become confident wrong findings. |
| idle-lane-audit.md:92 | `.claude/rules/idle-lane-audit.md:12` | reworded | - **Do not manufacture work.** If the audit is clean, say so and stop. |

| lane-autonomy.md:9 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:9` | archive-only | ## The rule (behavior-changing, every lane and loop) |
| lane-autonomy.md:11 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | **A lane only BLOCKS on a question when the answer is genuinely Zaal's to give. |
| lane-autonomy.md:11 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:11` | archive-only | Everything else: take the recommended option, log it, keep working.** |
| lane-autonomy.md:14 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | A question is ZAAL-GATED (block and wait) when ANY of these is true: |
| lane-autonomy.md:15 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | - money moves, or spend is committed |
| lane-autonomy.md:16 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | - anything goes PUBLIC or outbound (post, send, publish, DM) |
| lane-autonomy.md:17 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | - the action is IRREVERSIBLE (delete, on-chain, permaweb, key rotation) |
| lane-autonomy.md:18 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | - the answer is a FACT ONLY ZAAL KNOWS (who a person is, which file he |
| lane-autonomy.md:19 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:19` | archive-only | meant, what he intends) - guessing here is fabrication, not autonomy |
| lane-autonomy.md:20 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:5` | reworded | - Zaal explicitly asked to be asked |
| lane-autonomy.md:22 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:7` | reworded | Everything else - scope picks, ordering, which reversible approach, whether to include a PR-only sub-task - is NOT a gate: |
| lane-autonomy.md:24 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:24` | archive-only | Take the RECOMMENDED option (the one you would have listed first). |
| lane-autonomy.md:25 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:9` | reworded | LOG the choice where Zaal will see it: one line in the lane's report + |
| lane-autonomy.md:27 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:27` | archive-only | Zaal overrides at his next tap window; every auto-taken |
| lane-autonomy.md:28 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:28` | archive-only | choice must be cheap to reverse - that is what made it non-gated. |
| lane-autonomy.md:32 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:12` | reworded | - When in doubt whether something is gated, IT IS GATED. |
| lane-autonomy.md:32 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:32` | archive-only | The bar for |
| lane-autonomy.md:33 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:33` | archive-only | auto-proceed is "obviously reversible and obviously not Zaal's fact". |
| lane-autonomy.md:34 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:34` | archive-only | - Auto-proceed never stacks onto outbound: a non-gated scope choice that |
| lane-autonomy.md:36 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:12` | reworded | - The log line is mandatory. |
| lane-autonomy.md:36 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:36` | archive-only | An unlogged auto-choice is indistinguishable |
| lane-autonomy.md:37 | `archive/lane-autonomy.md:37` | archive-only | from drift (state-claims.md: name the source of every decision). |
| lane-autonomy.md:38 | `.claude/rules/lane-autonomy.md:12` | reworded | - This does not change AskUserQuestion in INTERACTIVE sessions where Zaal |

| liveness-probe-guard.md:3 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:3` | archive-only | Then it will kill the replacement. |

| liveness-probe-guard.md:10 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:3` | reworded | ## The three instances (all confirmed, ZAOOS#3065) |

| liveness-probe-guard.md:12 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:12` | archive-only | All three were hit by the [retired partner] lane, in `[retired partner]/scripts/refresh-fund.mjs`. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:15 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:15` | archive-only | **2026-07-13.** `browse restart` on an already-healthy session repeatedly |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:16 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:16` | archive-only | triggered "crashed twice in a row". |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:16 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:17` | reworded | Worked around at the call site: |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:16 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:16` | archive-only | `refresh-fund.mjs` stopped calling `restart` and started verifying the URL after `goto` instead. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:19 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:19` | archive-only | **2026-08-12.** The same crash-loop, *without* anything calling `restart` - it |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:20 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:20` | archive-only | happened inside the binary's own retry logic during a plain `goto`. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:20 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:20` | archive-only | Same signature, different trigger path. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:20 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:12` | reworded | Issue filed. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:22 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:22` | archive-only | **2026-08-17.** Reproduced with **0 orphans on the machine beforehand**, which |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:23 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:23` | archive-only | settles that orphans are downstream of the bug, not a precondition for it. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | **Root-caused 2026-08-14 by the ignite-radio lane** (investigating instance 2 - analysis, not a fourth occurrence). |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | Nothing was crashing. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | `ensureServer()` probes `/health` with a single `AbortSignal.timeout(2000)`. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | Measured latency on the *same* server: |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | **0.007s idle vs 2.02s and 3.92s while a heavy page loads** - a ~150x degradation that crosses the budget. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | On probe failure it calls `startServer()`, which `unlinkSync`s the state file and spawns a replacement **without killing the original**. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | So: busy server misread as dead -> state file deleted -> second server spawns on a new port at `about:blank` -> the page silently vanishes -> the original keeps running, orphaned, holding its Chromium. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | Repeat per command. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | Falsifiable prediction, confirmed: |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:25 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:25` | archive-only | **7 orphaned servers and 36 headless Chromium** before cleanup. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:37 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:37` | archive-only | Upstream fixed it in gstack 1.62.0.0 (`probeHealthWithBackoff`, their issue \#1781); local is 0.9.2.0. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:37 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:37` | archive-only | Their own code comment describes this exact failure - independent confirmation of the diagnosis. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:41 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:3` | reworded | ## The one principle |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:43 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:43` | archive-only | **A single short-timeout probe measures load, not liveness.** "No answer within N ms" and "dead" are different claims, and only the second one justifies killing something. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:47 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:47` | archive-only | ## Rules (behavior-changing) |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:49 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:7` | reworded | **A liveness probe retries with backoff before declaring death.** One timed |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:50 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:5` | reworded | probe is a load measurement. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:50 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:7` | reworded | Use several attempts spaced apart (upstream's `probeHealthWithBackoff(port, attempts=3, backoffMs=250)` is the reference shape). |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:50 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:50` | archive-only | If you are writing the probe, this is the whole fix. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:54 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:8` | reworded | **Never spawn a replacement without killing the original.** If a supervisor |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:55 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:55` | archive-only | concludes a process is dead and it is wrong, the cost must be one wasted check, not an orphan holding a browser. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:58 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:58` | archive-only | **Deleting shared state on a failed probe is how you lose the thing you were |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:59 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:59` | archive-only | checking on.** `unlinkSync(stateFile)` before the old process is confirmed gone destroys the only handle to a service that is still running fine. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:62 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:10` | reworded | **When a tool reports "crashed", check whether anything actually crashed.** |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:63 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:63` | archive-only | Instance 3 was titled "crash-loops" for two days. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:63 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:63` | archive-only | Nothing crashed. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:63 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:10` | reworded | Look for changing PIDs, accumulating processes, and a working first command followed by a failing second - that pattern is a supervisor problem, not a crash. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:67 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:11` | reworded | **Do not tune the timeout up and call it fixed.** A bigger budget moves the |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:68 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:68` | archive-only | threshold; it does not make the probe able to distinguish the two states. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:68 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:68` | archive-only | The number was never the bug. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:71 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:15` | reworded | ## The companion clause: an empty result is a failure |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:73 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:73` | archive-only | Adjacent shape, same session. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:73 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:73` | archive-only | A fetch can return **HTTP 200, a real final URL, no exception, and zero content.** Code that trusts the status records "the source has no data" instead of "I could not read the source" - and that lie propagates into a... |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:78 | `.claude/rules/liveness-probe-guard.md:15` | reworded | **Assert on content, not on status.** For any scrape or fetch whose output feeds a number someone will act on, treat `length == 0` (or below a sane floor) as a hard failure. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:78 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:78` | archive-only | The guard is one line and it is correct regardless of cause. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:82 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:82` | archive-only | Honest scope: on 2026-08-17 one lane observed a zero-length 200 from `[retired partner]` and **could not reproduce it in six subsequent runs across three configurations**, while a second lane never reproduced it at all. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:82 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:82` | archive-only | So this is **not** recorded as a property of that site - it is recorded because the guard costs nothing and the failure mode is real wherever it occurs. |
| liveness-probe-guard.md:82 | `archive/liveness-probe-guard.md:82` | archive-only | `silent-failure-guard.md` rule 2 states the general form ("assert the thing it was supposed to produce exists and is non-empty"); this is that rule pointed specifically at fetches that feed figures. |

| loop-evals.md:16 | `archive/loop-evals.md:16` | archive-only | ## Why (Karpathy, well-sourced; the two exact X links Zaal sent 402'd - UNVERIFIED) |
| loop-evals.md:18 | `archive/loop-evals.md:18` | archive-only | - **March of nines.** End-to-end success is p^n. |
| loop-evals.md:18 | `.claude/rules/loop-evals.md:18` | reworded | A 95%-per-step loop over 10 steps |
| loop-evals.md:19 | `archive/loop-evals.md:19` | archive-only | succeeds ~59% of the time. |
| loop-evals.md:19 | `archive/loop-evals.md:19` | archive-only | You cannot buy reliability with a better model alone - you buy it by gating each step. |
| loop-evals.md:19 | `archive/loop-evals.md:19` | archive-only | So each loop step that can be wrong gets a check. |
| loop-evals.md:21 | `.claude/rules/loop-evals.md:3` | reworded | - **Agents bloat and copy-paste.** Karpathy's loudest complaint: agents "implement an |
| loop-evals.md:22 | `archive/loop-evals.md:22` | archive-only | inefficient, bloated, brittle construction over 1000 lines" where 100 would do, duplicate blocks, and leave dead code. |

| loop-evals.md:124 | `archive/loop-evals.md:124` | archive-only | They were never indistinguishable - the subprocess call discarded a returncode that says which. |
| loop-evals.md:124 | `archive/loop-evals.md:124` | archive-only | But the reasoning was RIGHT THERE and internally consistent, so the reviewer read it, found it sound, and stopped. |
| loop-evals.md:128 | `.claude/rules/loop-evals.md:28` | reworded | > **A comment that explains why a shortcut is safe is the most effective thing |
| loop-evals.md:129 | `.claude/rules/loop-evals.md:28` | reworded | > there is at stopping a reviewer checking**, because the reasoning is right |
| loop-evals.md:130 | `archive/loop-evals.md:130` | archive-only | > there and coherent. |
| loop-evals.md:130 | `archive/loop-evals.md:130` | archive-only | Coherent-and-false beats absent. |
| loop-evals.md:132 | `archive/loop-evals.md:132` | archive-only | An absent justification invites the question. |
| loop-evals.md:132 | `archive/loop-evals.md:132` | archive-only | A present, plausible, wrong one answers it before it is asked. |
| loop-evals.md:132 | `archive/loop-evals.md:132` | archive-only | So a comment asserting that something cannot be distinguished, cannot happen, or is safe by construction is the highest-value thing in a diff to verify, not the thing that lets you skip verifying. |

| loop-evals.md:139 | `.claude/rules/loop-evals.md:26` | reworded | **Name both directions before reviewing a gate or filter.** Write down what |

| measurement-traps.md:310 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:17` | reworded | **The rule.** Before crediting a person, a lane, or a source with a specific claim: quote it, or attribute it loosely. |

| measurement-traps.md:319 | `archive/measurement-traps.md:319` | archive-only | Before reporting any measured claim, ask **which of these seven shapes am I in**: |
| measurement-traps.md:321 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Is this figure an average over a lifetime, or an interval? |
| measurement-traps.md:322 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:6` | reworded | Have I run my detector against a known-good input? |
| measurement-traps.md:323 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:7` | reworded | Am I reading source, or runtime? |
| measurement-traps.md:325 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Is something supervising the thing I just changed? |
| measurement-traps.md:326 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Does this provider actually observe what I am asking it to attribute? |
| measurement-traps.md:327 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Did I assert on content, or on a status code? |
| measurement-traps.md:328 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Is a quieting flag sitting between me and the measurement? |
| measurement-traps.md:329 | `archive/measurement-traps.md:329` | archive-only | Was the change global while I only checked the local case? |
| measurement-traps.md:330 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Did I write the conclusion before the command ran? |
| measurement-traps.md:331 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Did my check leave anything behind that I now want to delete? |
| measurement-traps.md:332 | `.claude/rules/measurement-traps.md:21` | reworded | Am I crediting a claim I could not quote? |

| noisy-signal-guard.md:3 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:3` | archive-only | Established 2026-08-07 after hitting the same failure FOUR times in one session, in four different disguises. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:3 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:3` | archive-only | `silent-failure-guard.md` covers a system that reports success while doing nothing. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:3 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:3` | archive-only | This is its mirror: a system that reports failure so often that the real failure hides inside the noise. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:8 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:8` | archive-only | Both end the same way - a human stops reading the output. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:10 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:10` | archive-only | ## The four instances (all real, all the same day) |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:12 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:12` | archive-only | **35 false-positive `public` route labels.** The surface-map detector knew two |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:13 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:13` | archive-only | auth guards and dumped everything else into `public`. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:13 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:13` | archive-only | The four highest-risk routes it flagged were all correctly guarded. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:13 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:13` | archive-only | A flag that fires 35 times gets ignored on the one that matters. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:16 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:16` | archive-only | **A review flag that could never reach zero.** After fixing the real leaks, |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:17 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:17` | archive-only | three correctly-public routes stayed flagged forever. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:17 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:17` | archive-only | Permanent non-zero is indistinguishable from broken, so `@public-reviewed` was added specifically so the count CAN reach zero. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:20 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:20` | archive-only | **183 typecheck errors on a healthy repo.** `bot/node_modules` was empty on |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:21 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:21` | archive-only | this machine, so 143 of them were phantom. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:21 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:21` | archive-only | That trained me to measure "no NEW errors" instead of "no errors" - and three real bugs in live source sat inside the noise for who knows how long, including a listener leak. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:24 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:24` | archive-only | **A CONTRA loop over a four-note vault** would have invented collisions and |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:25 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:25` | archive-only | taught its reader to skip its output. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:25 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:14` | reworded | Not shipped, for exactly this reason. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:27 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:27` | archive-only | ## The rule (behavior-changing) |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:29 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:5` | reworded | **A signal must be able to reach zero, and must not fire on the expected case.** Before shipping any check, flag, count, or alert, answer both: |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:32 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:7` | reworded | **Can it reach zero?** If there is a legitimate state that keeps it non-zero |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:33 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:33` | archive-only | forever, the check is a permanent alarm - which is no alarm. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:33 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:7` | reworded | Give it a way to be cleared: an explicit acknowledgement marker (`@public-reviewed`), an allowlist, or a narrower rule. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:33 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:7` | reworded | An acknowledgement asserts *a human looked*, not *this is safe*. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:37 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:8` | reworded | **Does it fire on the normal case?** Measure the actual firing rate against |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:38 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:38` | archive-only | Four false positives in a row is not bad luck, it is a broken instrument. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:38 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:8` | reworded | Fix the instrument before acting on its output. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:41 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:10` | reworded | **And when a check is loud, do not adapt to it - fix it.** The tell that you have already adapted is measuring a DELTA instead of an ABSOLUTE: |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:41 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:10` | reworded | "no new errors", "no new warnings", "same failures as main". |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:41 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:10` | reworded | Those are legitimate only when you have also established what the baseline IS and why. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:41 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:41` | archive-only | Otherwise the baseline is where the real problems live. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:49 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:12` | reworded | - **Are the dependencies actually installed?** An empty `node_modules` produces |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:50 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:50` | archive-only | hundreds of confident, entirely fake errors. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:50 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:50` | archive-only | Verify the toolchain resolves before believing anything it says (`ls node_modules \| wc -l`, not just "the command ran"). |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:53 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:53` | archive-only | - **Does CI agree?** CI installs cleanly from a lockfile. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:53 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:12` | reworded | If CI is green and local |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:54 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:12` | reworded | is red, the environment is the bug - not the repo. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:54 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:5` | reworded | Check CI before diagnosing the codebase. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:56 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:12` | reworded | - **Is the baseline you are diffing against current?** A baseline captured before |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:57 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:57` | archive-only | an intervening merge will show phantom deltas. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:57 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:57` | archive-only | Re-measure against the CURRENT target, not a stale file. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:62 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:14` | reworded | - This does NOT argue for fewer checks. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:62 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:14` | reworded | It argues for checks that mean something |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:63 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:63` | archive-only | when they fire. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:63 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:63` | archive-only | A quiet, accurate check beats a loud, approximate one. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:64 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:64` | archive-only | - A check that legitimately has findings is not noise - noise is a check whose |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:65 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:65` | archive-only | findings are mostly wrong, or that can never be resolved. |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:66 | `.claude/rules/noisy-signal-guard.md:14` | reworded | - Reporting "clean" honestly is a success, not a wasted run |
| noisy-signal-guard.md:67 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:67` | archive-only | (`anti-fabrication.md` rule 4: grade to the lowest severity the evidence supports). |

| noisy-signal-guard.md:72 | `archive/noisy-signal-guard.md:72` | archive-only | 2026-08-07, four instances in one session: the surface-map detector (PR #2950), the review flag that could not reach zero (#2953), the 183-phantom-error typecheck that hid three real bugs (#2963), and the CONTRA loop ... |

| pii-hygiene.md:9 | `.claude/rules/pii-hygiene.md:3` | reworded | The risk is **leakage of third-party personal data** through: |
| pii-hygiene.md:11 | `archive/pii-hygiene.md:11` | archive-only | **Git commit** - PII written into a research doc, recap, or memory file that lands on a public repo. |

| pii-hygiene.md:17 | `archive/pii-hygiene.md:17` | archive-only | The blast radius is reputational + relational, not financial. |
| pii-hygiene.md:17 | `archive/pii-hygiene.md:17` | archive-only | The fix is **default-private**: query output stays off-repo, summaries omit names unless authorized, and the perimeter catches accidents before push. |

| pii-hygiene.md:198 | `.claude/rules/pii-hygiene.md:26` | reworded | If he then asks "save that to a doc," redact PII per Rule 3 before writing. |

| pii-hygiene.md:233 | `.claude/rules/pii-hygiene.md:36` | reworded | - **Never** dump raw email or calendar invite content into a Bonfire episode. |
| pii-hygiene.md:233 | `.claude/rules/pii-hygiene.md:36` | reworded | Episode bodies are natural-language prose summaries, not data dumps. |

| pre-merge-security-and-suite.md:36 | `archive/pre-merge-security-and-suite.md:36` | archive-only | The orchestrator READS the route's auth before merging. |

| research-grounding.md:14 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:5` | reworded | **A cited source is not grounding unless it was actually fetched and read.** Every specific claim, number, quote, URL, or repo in a research output traces to a page the agent fetched THIS run - or it is marked UNVERIF... |
| research-grounding.md:14 | `archive/research-grounding.md:14` | archive-only | This mirrors Anthropic Hermes's "grounded-citations" (claims fact-checked against actual page text) and ZAO's Proof Drops (a claim anchored to quotable evidence). |
| research-grounding.md:19 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | ## RAW TEXT, NOT A SUMMARY - the WebFetch trap (added 2026-08-08) |
| research-grounding.md:21 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:10` | reworded | A fetch is not automatically grounding. |
| research-grounding.md:21 | `archive/research-grounding.md:21` | archive-only | **`WebFetch` does not return the page** - its own description says it "answers `prompt` against it **using a small fast model**". |
| research-grounding.md:21 | `archive/research-grounding.md:21` | archive-only | You receive that model's answer about text you never saw, so every quote, number and name in it is recall, not source. |
| research-grounding.md:26 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | - **Never quote from WebFetch.** Attribute it as a summary or do not use it. |
| research-grounding.md:27 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | - **Load-bearing claims come from RAW text**: |
| research-grounding.md:27 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | `curl` plus an HTML strip, an |
| research-grounding.md:28 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | official JSON API, or ZAO's keyless fetchers (`zao-fetch-reddit.sh`, `zao-fetch-farcaster.sh`, FxTwitter) - all of which return raw by construction. |
| research-grounding.md:30 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | - **State the METHOD in Sources**, not just FULL/PARTIAL/FAILED, so a reader can |
| research-grounding.md:31 | `archive/research-grounding.md:31` | archive-only | tell a verbatim quote from a reconstructed one. |
| research-grounding.md:32 | `archive/research-grounding.md:32` | archive-only | - **The same trap applies to subagents.** A subagent's prose report IS a summary. |
| research-grounding.md:33 | `.claude/rules/research-grounding.md:7` | reworded | If its specifics matter, have it write the raw text to a file and return the PATH - then quote from disk (`anti-fabrication.md` rule 1). |
| research-grounding.md:35 | `archive/research-grounding.md:35` | archive-only | - WebFetch stays fine for triage: does this page exist, what is it broadly about. |
| research-grounding.md:36 | `archive/research-grounding.md:36` | archive-only | The line is whether a sentence will be quoted, carry a number, or support a decision. |
| research-grounding.md:39 | `archive/research-grounding.md:39` | archive-only | Full audit and the community source: doc 2250. |

| session-boundaries.md:10 | `.claude/rules/session-boundaries.md:3` | reworded | The failure is not length. |
| session-boundaries.md:10 | `.claude/rules/session-boundaries.md:3` | reworded | It is **context conflict**: holding superseded state alongside current state until the two are indistinguishable. |
| session-boundaries.md:10 | `archive/session-boundaries.md:10` | archive-only | Every wrong claim in that session was a state or absence claim made while a stale version of the same fact was still in context: |

| silent-failure-guard.md:8 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:8` | archive-only | ## The three that motivated this (2026-07-27, all confirmed) |
| silent-failure-guard.md:10 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:10` | archive-only | **Admin bypass, live in prod.** cocconcertz.com accepted the literal cookie |
| silent-failure-guard.md:11 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:11` | archive-only | value `admin` as full admin. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:11 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:6` | reworded | It 200'd normally. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:11 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:11` | archive-only | One curl header = delete events, message all users, change roles. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:13 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:13` | archive-only | **Cron green for 7 weeks while doing nothing.** An auto-close job 503'd every |
| silent-failure-guard.md:14 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:14` | archive-only | run (its key was in GitHub but never in Vercel), yet the CI step piped `curl \| tee`, so bash reported `tee`'s exit 0 - green for ~7 weeks. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:16 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:16` | archive-only | **Test suite that never ran.** `vitest.config.ts` failed to load under Node |
| silent-failure-guard.md:17 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:17` | archive-only | 20 (ERR_REQUIRE_ESM); the job passed anyway. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:17 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:17` | archive-only | Renaming to `.mts` turned 0 real tests into 149. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:20 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:20` | archive-only | Sibling case: a Cloudinary `api.ping()` that returns `{status:"ok"}` regardless of whether the key actually has read/create scope - it masked a 24-day permission outage. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:24 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:24` | archive-only | ## The rules (behavior-changing) |
| silent-failure-guard.md:26 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:5` | reworded | **Verify the real EFFECT, not the wrapper's exit code.** A pipeline's exit |
| silent-failure-guard.md:27 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:27` | archive-only | is the LAST command's. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:27 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:27` | archive-only | Never gate on a piped command without `set -o pipefail`, and prefer asserting the actual result (the HTTP status, the row count, the file's existence) over any exit code. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:30 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:6` | reworded | **A 200 / exit 0 is not proof of success.** For an endpoint, assert the |
| silent-failure-guard.md:31 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:6` | reworded | status explicitly (`curl -fsS` or check `-w '%{http_code}'`). |
| silent-failure-guard.md:31 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:6` | reworded | For a job, assert the thing it was supposed to produce exists and is non-empty. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:33 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:7` | reworded | **A missing tool is a FAIL, not a pass.** If the verifier (esbuild, the test |
| silent-failure-guard.md:34 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:34` | archive-only | runner, a scanner) can't load or isn't installed, the step must exit non-zero and BLOCK - never fall through green. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:34 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:34` | archive-only | (Extends agent-loops.md rule 30, vacuous-verify.) |
| silent-failure-guard.md:37 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:8` | reworded | **`\|\| true` never goes on a GATE or a SECURITY check.** It is fine on a grep |
| silent-failure-guard.md:38 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:8` | reworded | whose no-match is expected, or an optional platform-specific install. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:38 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:8` | reworded | It is banned on anything whose failure should stop the line - secret scans, doc guards, migrations, deploy verifies. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:41 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:41` | archive-only | **Security scans fail closed.** A secret/PII scan that errors must ABORT, not |
| silent-failure-guard.md:42 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:42` | archive-only | pass. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:42 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:9` | reworded | `DIFF=$(git diff ... \|\| true)` that yields empty-on-failure trivially passes the scan - use `set -o pipefail` and check the scanner ran. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:44 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:10` | reworded | **A "soft-fail" must be LOUD, not silent.** Returning `{ok:true}` when a DB |
| silent-failure-guard.md:45 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:10` | reworded | write failed is acceptable UX only if the failure is logged at error level AND surfaced (alert / metric) - otherwise it is silent data loss. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:45 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:45` | archive-only | Prefer a distinct status the caller can see, or at minimum a monitored error log. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:48 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:11` | reworded | **Health/ping checks assert their dependencies.** A health endpoint that |
| silent-failure-guard.md:49 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:11` | reworded | returns 200 without checking the DB / the key's real scope / the queue is theater. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:49 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:49` | archive-only | Check the actual capability the check claims to prove. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:53 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:53` | archive-only | target.** "SESSION_SECRET unset = no auth, trust everyone" is fine for a local CLI and is the default state of every fresh deployment, so it is the branch that ships unless someone remembers a manual step. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:53 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:12` | reworded | Any such branch asserts it is not on the deploy target (`VERCEL`, `CI`, a hostname) and fails closed there - for reads as well as writes. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:53 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:53` | archive-only | Added 2026-09-15: z.thezao.xyz ran 63 days with anonymous owner-level write access because the hatch documented as "local CLI unchanged" had no host check (ZAOOS issue: |
| silent-failure-guard.md:53 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:53` | archive-only | "a security gate is off on the deployed host", 2nd instance; the 1st is case 1 above). |
| silent-failure-guard.md:64 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:64` | archive-only | pasted into the doc.** Reading the code proves the code; running the tests proves the tests; neither proves the deployment, whose configuration lives somewhere they never look. |
| silent-failure-guard.md:64 | `archive/silent-failure-guard.md:64` | archive-only | The July audit of zaalcaster wrote "This is FIXED and LIVE" from the source and was right about the case it named and blind to the one that was deployed. |

| silent-failure-guard.md:73 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:15` | reworded | - Did I assert the real effect, or just an exit code / status? |
| silent-failure-guard.md:74 | `.claude/rules/silent-failure-guard.md:15` | reworded | - Would this still go green if the underlying thing were completely broken? |

| state-claims.md:3 | `archive/state-claims.md:3` | archive-only | Established 2026-08-08 after a single session produced EIGHT wrong statements about this repo's own state. |
| state-claims.md:3 | `archive/state-claims.md:3` | archive-only | Not predictions, not opinions - claims about files sitting on the disk. |
| state-claims.md:3 | `archive/state-claims.md:3` | archive-only | Zaal: |
| state-claims.md:3 | `archive/state-claims.md:3` | archive-only | "There's a lot of times u say correction, saying it's built. |
